- **Full fix-chain hash map** (rows 28+ = the vanilla-plus feature era, live; rows 1–27 = the glide/D3D chain, moved verbatim to HISTORY H21; per-fix verdicts: §3ter + HISTORY H22, narrative HISTORY H17):

  | fix | commit | subject | note |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | — | `4857c30` → `00d97a0` | **rows 1–27 moved to HISTORY.md H21** | the glide/D3D fix chain, merge-era and 0.0.1/0.0.2 rows, verbatim; §3 one-liners still cover every fix |
  | 28 | `dec7a22` | vanilla-plus feature: alt-key item-label toggle | **FEATURE, not a fix** — config-gated QoL, default OFF = vanilla hold byte-for-byte (ledger rows 25-27 above — inserted in the 0.0.2 docs pass). D2R "Show Items: Toggle" parity: each full Alt down→up cycle flips ground-item-label visibility instead of hold-to-show. Config: `d2vp.ini` next to d2.exe, `[features] alt_toggle=1` (GetPrivateProfileIntA, exe-dir anchored, re-read at most every 2 s via GetTickCount; missing file/section/key/garbage = 0). All writers converge the flag to the latch in toggle mode: `CMD_SetHoldAlt` (keydown flips), `CMD_ReleaseHoldAltAndClearSelection` (keyup re-asserts), `HandleMouseButtonEvent` non-zero-event branch, and `InitializeUI` resets the latch. **Implicit fact worth keeping: the 0x1c registration fires `HandleMouseButtonEvent`'s non-zero-event branch on clicks AND app-activation — vanilla uses it to clear labels on click-while-holding-Alt (the "labels vanish until re-press" quirk); in toggle mode it converges to the latch, which is also what heals a stuck Alt after a lost keyup across alt-tab.** Repeat-safe by construction: CmdTbl.cpp already filters lParam bit-30 auto-repeat upstream, so keydown fires exactly once per physical press; no guard needed in the handlers. `CUnit.cpp` item-hit-test exclusion and the Draw.cpp tooltip branch read the same flag unchanged — latched mode behaves like vanilla "Alt permanently held" |
  | 29 | `9cf9f69` | vanilla-plus feature addendum: auto-create d2vp.ini template | **FEATURE addendum to row 28, not a fix** — a missing `d2vp.ini` is now created with a documented all-defaults template (comment header + `[features] alt_toggle=0`) so the opt-in keys are discoverable at the title screen without docs. One attempt per process, from two hooks: client boot `ApplicationMain` (D2Client/Engine/Application.cpp, runs before the title screen) and lazily inside `ALTTOGGLE_Active` before the first config read (covers flows that skip the boot hook). CREATE_NEW after a GetFileAttributesA existence check, so a file racing into existence between check and create is never clobbered; any failure (read-only dir, AV lock) stays silent and config-absence remains the vanilla path; a file deleted while the game runs is NOT re-fought (next start recreates it). New statics: gbAltIniAttempted + helpers ALTTOGGLE_BuildIniPath (refuses the unreachable dir-less GetModuleFileNameA case instead of the old CWD-relative fallback — strictly safer) / ALTTOGGLE_WriteIniTemplate; public decl `ALTTOGGLE_EnsureIni` in D2Client.h. ## 3. The eleven defects (root causes, one line each) — the fix-chain record (upstream verdict: §3ter)

1. **Phantom texture-cache structs** — function-local statics `gGlideSmallTextureCacheBase`/`gnGlideState` were `.data` labels adjacent to `gGlideSmallTextureCache[3]`; rebuilt they're unrelated 4-byte objects → every upload addressed a phantom → `0xc1` halt (`-3dfx` launcher crash, issue #5).
2. **1-based size class as 0-based index** — class 3 indexed one `0x20` struct past `cache[3]`. Clamped + decremented.
3. **`GLTEXCACHE_EnsureCapacity` result discarded** — caller stored an uninitialized local as the 1-based node index. Now returns the index.
4. **Stale sprite-header slots splice free-list nodes onto the active list** — recycled headers inherited stale node indices; promote walked the node's own links without `nUsedCount++` → cache drained → `0xc1` with a clean-looking free list. Fixed by backpointer/mode validation.
5. **Scattered fan-vertex globals → giant clipped triangle** — one `4 × 0x1c` vertex block decompiled into ~20 globals; linker scatters them. All quad/tile submits now use contiguous local blocks with every field filled.
6. **Fan slot order + portrait spans OOB** — decompiler names offset one fan slot (hardware order `v0=TL, v1=BL, v2=BR, v3=TR`); portrait (negative-aspect) spans indexed backward out of `gaPerspectiveImageSizeTable`. Negative classes compute spans from pow2 dims (`nTexW/H·2^(8−lod)`).
7. **Wall/shadow tile fans** — 8 scattered "vertex globals"; `oow/s/t` presets were `InitPerspectiveTables` function-local statics, unreachable after rebuild; `ApplyColorLighting` writes landed on unknown `.bss`. Both `DrawWallTileSimple`/`DrawShadowTileSimple` now submit contiguous blocks with full-texture mapping.
8. **Wall-subtile cache halt** — `GLSUBTILE_EnsureCapacity` decompiled as `void`, dropping its live 1-based index → `ERROR_Halt 0x112` at end-of-loading. Restored the `short` return.
9. **Lightmap falloff table truncated** — `gnLightmapInterpolationState` declared `[256]`, live index 0x100 → saturated cells multiply rgb by 0 → **black light cores / black unit silhouettes**. Two-line fix: `[257]`.
10. **Ground-tile vertex colors through a `(float)` cast** — stores the *f32 bit pattern* of the intended PARGB (`0xff808080` → `0x4f7f8080`). Fix: 9 stores via `*(uint32_t*)&`.
11. **Shadow-quad portrait spans** — `DrawShadowQuad` read spans with the negative aspect → every unit shadow sampled texel (0,0). Fix: mirror fix 6's span block. Caught by the ShadowDiag capture after static analysis *passed* the path.

### 3bis. Sound series — status block only (full record: HISTORY H3)

- **Defect 12 (title/in-game music silent)** — CLOSED, fix `4031fa7` (R6): Ghidra FP bit-pattern constants decoded in `D2SoundSmp.cpp` clamps + `-2000` dB scale restore. Field-verified.
- **Defect 13 (NPC/monster/player SFX silent)** — CLOSED, fix 16 `9c8beb9`: `SOUND_UpdateChannelParams` reads `SOUNDSMP_CalculateVolumeAndPan`'s integer out params as `uint32_t` (was float-storage punning → subnormal truncation → vol 0 → att=-10000). Music channels skip that block — the observed music-vs-SFX split. Field-verified.
- **Upstreamed** as jaenster/redivivus **PR #10** (3 commits, self-contained comments; upstream additionally lacked the `-2000.0` scale fix). **SMP layer exonerated against retail decompiles — do not re-audit.** sounddiag retired (HISTORY H4).

### 3ter. Upstream verdict on the glide defects — PR #6 closed by jaenster (2026-10-27)

jaenster reviewed the glide defects against 1.14d and fixed the confirmed ones **in the Ghidra database** rather than merging the C++ — the next regen emits the right code (fixes at the source of truth, no regen conflicts). His verdict, mapped to the §3 defect numbering:

| Defects | Verdict (jaenster, 2026-10-27) |
|---|---|
| 1, 6/11, 9, 10 | **Confirmed, fixed in Ghidra**: the vertex block and the texture caches as one object; the span table as one `int[14]`; the falloff table with 257 entries; the ground vertex colour as an integer store. |
| 3, 5, 7, 8 | **Already fixed** in Ghidra after the tree the PR was worked from (`eef5f2c`). |
| 2 | **Rejected**: 1.14d uses the 1-based size class directly; the `− 1` only undid a rebase from the same patch. |
| 4 | **Rejected**: 1.14d has no slot check there — the original's behaviour is kept. |

Implications for vanilla-plus:

- The hand fixes for defects 1, 3, 5, 6/7/8, 9, 10/11 will be **superseded (or conflict) when the next upstream export lands**. Merge plan: take upstream's regen, drop the matching hand fixes, re-run the §12 gate set + the §11 field loop.
- **Defects 2 and 4: vanilla-plus keeps its fixes until proven unnecessary.** jaenster's verdicts describe the Ghidra *source*; the crashes those two fixes address are relink-layout phenomena (adjacency/scattering after relink) — a retail-faithful regen can still relink differently. Re-verify empirically post-export before dropping.

## 5. Closed investigations

→ **HISTORY.md H5** (do-not-reopen list with the evidence trail). The one standing rule from it: **the ShadowDiag capture caught what static analysis could not** — trust captures over audits.

## 6. Methodology — what actually worked (keep doing this)

1. **Evidence-first per the charter**: never author a fix from static reading alone when a capture can pin the stage (ShadowDiag is the flagship; LightDiag v1 turned "units render black" into a two-line fix).
2. **Retail A/B through the logging wrapper** — same wrapper build, same scene, diff the streams. Budget-limited dumps mean early-frame evidence only.
3. **Byte-level forensics over narrative**: file sizes, `cmp`, f32-bit-pattern math, crc groups, per-record parser asserts.
4. **One-shot client diagnostics with a decision tree shipped in the overlay** — the dump should discriminate between candidate stages in a single build.
5. **QA gauntlet for every delivery** (§7) — non-negotiable.

## 7. Delivery conventions (condensed; full texts + fix-11 lesson: HISTORY H8)

- **Patch-first, always.** Full-file drop-ins are fallback only, and must be cut from the tip the user actually has (base-state grep gate printed in the delivery doc). The fix-11 regression (HISTORY H8) is why.
- **QA gauntlet**: apply round-trip byte-identical on every supported base; negative test refuses cleanly on a fixed tree; unique anchors per edit site; `g++ -fsyntax-only` for new files; builders persisted as scripts.
- **Routes**: git bundle (history matters) → `git bundle create download/<name>.bundle origin/vanilla-plus..vanilla-plus`, verify in a scratch repo at the *fetched* prerequisite tip. Overlay zip (file-level) → only files actually changed + MANIFEST (base commit, sha256s, grep gates, steps); tmpfiles.org **60-min expiry**, post the extracted `/dl/` token link, byte-verify. format-patch (PR-style, proven on PR #10) → commits in a scratch worktree at the target base, `git format-patch`, `git apply --check` QA on pristine base, zip + `MANIFEST.sha256`.
- Sandbox cannot push — the user applies/commits/pushes on their tree (`F:\GAMES\Diablo II`), commits styled `vanilla-plus fix N: ...` / upstream plain-subject style.

## 8. Diagnostics on the tree (condensed; deep specs: HISTORY H4)

- **ShadowDiag** — in-tree (`D2Glide/Renderer/ShadowDiag.{h,cpp}` + 3 hooks in Glide.cpp: include, `DrawGroundTile` head `NoteWorldFrame()`, `DrawShadowQuad` tail `OnShadowQuad`). Gate: `grep -c "ShadowDiag" D2Glide/Renderer/Glide.cpp` → 3.
- **LightDiag v2** — in-tree (`D2Client/Draw/LightDiag.{h,cpp}` + hooks in `LightMap.cpp`, `D2Client.cpp:1802`, `Draw.cpp`); dumps at in-world frame 90.
- **DropDiag** — hooks in `Drop.cpp` (include + `NoteRollLookup`/`NoteItemGen`/`NoteItemSpawnAudit`). Gate: `grep -c "pSpawnRoomFound" D2Game/ITEMS/Items/Drop.cpp` → 3.
- **sounddiag/viddiag — RETIRED** (r10, `4857c30`; mark formats of record: HISTORY H4). Re-cut probes if sound symptoms return — don't reinvent.
- **`support/d2_boot.{h,cpp}` stays**: permanent `[d2boot]` boot/crash marks are load-bearing (12 files).
- All idle diag modules are zero-behavior-change; strip-or-env-gate is **offered, not requested** (keep §12 gates accurate if touched).

## 10. Tooling

→ **HISTORY.md H7** (build-spec table; scripts were lost in the 2026-10-26 sandbox reset — recreate on demand per the Script Persistence Rule). Sandbox currently holds `scripts/` from the rev-4+ era only.

## 11. Field-validation loop (how work gets done)

1. Author fix/diagnostic in the sandbox → package per §7 → deliver from `download/`.
2. User applies on their Windows tree (`F:\GAMES\Diablo II`), commits, pushes.
3. User builds locally on Win11 (`tools/build-local-win11.ps1`) or grabs the CI artifact (mingw-i686 back in service 2026-10-28, green on the 0.0.2 tag) — the local build remains the authoritative gate.
4. User reproduces the scene through the logging wrapper — rebuild **and** retail, same scene — and/or captures diag dumps.
5. Uploads logs/zips here → decode → decide next step from data, not theory.
6. Append a worklog Task entry **every round** (the worklog is the institutional memory — this handoff is just its index). Public artifacts worth keeping: releases on the fork, issues/PRs upstream.

## 12. Triage quick-paths (if a symptom returns)

| Symptom | First check |
|---|---|
| Black light cores / black unit silhouettes | falloff table still `[257]`? (`globals/globals.h:3075`) |
| Light radius / torch discoloration | `grep -c "vanilla-plus fix 3"` → 1 (zero-and-recompute at ~1042) and `grep -c "vanilla-plus fix 2"` → 1 (ported stale-slot validation at ~1071-1098) in `D2Glide/Renderer/Glide.cpp`. Missing = a fix-11-style regression (§7 routes). |
| Every unit shadow is a solid blob / samples (0,0) | `grep -c "ShadowDiag" D2Glide/Renderer/Glide.cpp` → 3 and `grep -c "D2Client/D2GfxDataStrc.h" D2Glide/Renderer/ShadowDiag.cpp` → 1. Then a fresh `d2shadow-diag.txt` — aspect<0 records must show s 0..64/0..128, t 0..256 |
| Drops never broadcast / ground items vanish | `grep -c "pSpawnRoomFound" D2Game/ITEMS/Items/Drop.cpp` → 3 (upstream canonical fix 14) + DropDiag hooks intact. A `pResolvedSpawnRoom` reference = the 0fa2003 regression (deleted in 45c6c2d). |
| `0xc1` halt at boot/menu | texture cache (defects 1–4); check promote bails in bootlog; the fix-2 validation block must run in the upload path (`if (nNewCacheNode)` at ~1092) |
| Clean HALT `0x164/0x165/0x16d/0x16e/0x171/0x172/0x19a/0x19b/0x1b4/0x1b5/0x1c0` (D3D sprite upload) | fix-21 guards present? `grep -c "vanilla-plus fix 21" D2Direct3D/Renderer/Direct3D.cpp` → 2 (class-slot range guard ~:2481 + nodeIdx backpointer validation ~:2513). Missing = a fix-11-style regression. The 11 codes are all `UploadTextureToCache` branches (H12 footnote) — the halt code names the branch. **Fix 22 makes the wall self-reporting**: the `upload-halt <code>: ...` lines in d2boot.log (emitted just before the halt) carry mode/class/node/pool + the failing node's full contents — read them FIRST, then the halt code. Sanity anchors: pool max ≈ hundreds and nodes != 0 means seeded; a node with surf=00000000 inside a seeded pool = the round-9 contradiction, escalate with the dump. No `upload-halt` lines at all = a pre-fix-22 build (check for the `vanilla-plus fixset through fix 22` banner; local builds do not always rotate the commit hash). `fix22: free-callback skipped ... (was a wild write)` lines = the hardened callback catching live garbage pairs — include them in the report. **Fix 23 closes the 0x16d root cause** (typed-pointer scaling, H15) — **SUPERSEDED at the ad964f3 merge**: upstream's re-typed upload computes node addresses in INT arithmetic (`iCacheNodeIdx − 0x10 + base`, base = `aCaches[class−1].pNodeBuffer` via the `(cache − 0x4 + class·0x1c)` int read), which is byte-exact by construction; the old `grep "vanilla-plus fix 23" → 3` gate is RETIRED (0 hits is CORRECT now). A fired halt with a HEALTHY dump now means: audit upstream's NEW arithmetic for the failing premise before suspecting memory. **Fix 24 quick-paths**: BLACK ground/floor (D3D) = index-buffer statics — gate `grep -c "g_aD3DGroundTileStripIndices" D2Direct3D/Renderer/Direct3D.cpp` → 4 (def + comment refs + prefill + draw; re-ported at `8030526`); wrong-hue UI sprites/text = check the palette-buffer extents first (`gnPaletteIndexCounter` now **uint8_t[39]** — the true extent, 36B PALETTEENTRYs + 3B manual triple; upstream form + guard; if a `D2GUARD gnPaletteIndexCounter` line ever fires, the loader writes past 39 and the field analysis must be redone). |
| Halt `0x112` at end-of-loading | wall-subtile cache (defect 8): **gate RETIRED at the ad964f3 merge** — upstream re-typed `GLSUBTILE_EnsureCapacity` to return the live index; the old `nTexCacheIdx = (short int)nNodeIdx0` grep no longer applies. If 0x112 returns, audit upstream's `GLBLOCKS_LoadWallSubtileTexture` miss path first. |
| Black-triangle grid on title screen | fan slot order (defect 6) — do *not* blame the wrapper (attribution retracted) |
| Foreign-module crash in `d2boot.log` | crash-triage stack: VEH skip paths dump module+offset; symbolize via linker map |
| New "retail right, rebuild wrong" color issue | start from the §1 root pattern; audit decompiler guesses around the site; then capture (§6.1) |
| Title/in-game music silent (again) | defect 12 relapse: `rg '\(double\)0x[0-9a-f]{16}' D2Sound/Src/D2SoundSmp.cpp` must be empty. Probe marks retired — re-cut before assuming (HISTORY H4). |
| NPC/monster/player SFX silent, music fine | fix 16 relapse: `grep -c "vanilla-plus fix 16" D2Client/GAME/Sound.cpp` → 1 (the `uint32_t nCalcVolume / nCalcPan` block; a `(uint32_t*)&fFalloffMin` pun there = bug is back). SMP layer stays exonerated — do not re-audit. |

## 13. Sandbox gotchas

- No `gh` CLI/token — PR/issue body edits are handed to the user.
- `git apply` needs `--no-index` and `GIT_CEILING_DIRECTORIES=/home/z/my-project` (the clone has no clean parent boundary); note `git apply --no-index` OUTSIDE a repo silently SKIPS hunks with exit 0 — use a real scratch repo for QA.
- A dirty worktree means *new* local work: inspect it, don't blanket-revert.
- The sandbox resets between sessions — persist anything load-bearing in the repo itself (that is why this handoff and HISTORY.md are committed in-tree).
- Script Persistence Rule: scripts >10 lines go to `scripts/` and get edited in place on failure.
- **No mingw toolchain in the sandbox and no apt rights** — constants-level fixes are verified by diff review + curve math, not by compiling; the user's local Win11 build is the compile gate.
- tmpfiles.org links expire in **60 minutes**; always post the extracted `/dl/` token link and byte-verify.
- User timezone America/Toronto; session language English; delivery zips always to `/home/z/my-project/download/`.

Different loops, same party-regroup cluster, same corruption class. Decoding the dump also exposed a hangwatch symbolization bug: it printed offsets as `runtime_va - map_va`, inflated by exactly the relocation delta (`+0x490000` on the 0x890000 run, `+0xc0000` on the 0x4c0000 run — the map is linked at preferred base 0x400000; symbol NAMES were always correct, offsets were unreadable). Fixed: offsets are map-relative now, a relocated run logs a `hangwatch: note base ... = map ... + reloc ...` line, and the map-load line prints the symbol span for stale-map detection. **Critical finding — the fix overlays never reached the owner's build**: four symbols independently land on their exact old-map VAs (`GetUnitPartyId`@0x45f426, `PlayerLeft`@0x4609c1, `SETWARPDEST`@0x465470, `SCMD_ProcessIncomingPacketBuffer`@0x467771), the Game.cpp frames are RVA-identical across both dumps, and the boot string still reads 0.0.4 — the running exe is layout-identical to the pre-rev34 build, so rev34's queue lock and rev35's sanitize/bounds were never compiled in (most likely the zip-mtime/stale-obj trap: files extracted with old mtimes are skipped by the incremental build, or the zip was not extracted at all). **rev36 hardening (D2Client/GAME/Roster.cpp, 31 cap sites)**: every unbounded walk in the roster module now carries a 64-node cap + one `roster heal:` breadcrumb — regroup (GPIP outer iterations + party scan + collect), sorts (SortByName/SortByLevel outer+scan, SortPartyListsByPartyId outer+scan, SortAllParties), flatten (party loop + tail walk, which now CUTS the cycled input instead of relaying it), move-to-front walks, the gpRosterOther recycle scan, roster-link scans/frees, portal-owner/partied-roster/by-guid walks, ClearBothRosterLists, and all five free loops, which are **cycle-safe** (remember first-freed node, stop when the walk returns to it, zero pNext before freeing — the harness caught a vanilla double-free/UAF on a cycled party list here). Well-formed data (SP max 8 roster nodes) never reaches any cap, so vanilla behavior is unchanged — verified by the rev36 host harness `scripts/roster_harness_rev36.cpp`: 10 SP replays pass with **zero caps fired**, 4 adversarial injections (cycled player chain with sanitize unavailable, cycled pOwner chain = the second dump's spin site, cycled party list incl. the double-free case, cycled gpRosterOther) all terminate with the expected breadcrumbs, ASan-clean. **Build self-identification bumped to `vanilla-plus 0.0.5 (rev36)`** (D2Client/Engine/Application.cpp): the next d2boot.log MUST say 0.0.5 — if it still says 0.0.4 the overlay did not rebuild, and that is the first thing to check, before any code analysis. Gates: 22 new "d2boot-fix:" rev36 rows, suite **183/183**. Overlay zip: full cumulative set over the owner's rev33-era tree (13 rev35-zip files + `support/d2_hangwatch.cpp` + `D2Client/Engine/Application.cpp` + handoff/gates), fresh mtimes, `REV36-APPLY.txt` inside with the clean-rebuild instruction. — Rev 35 (2026-10-31, loading-freeze hangwatch round): the owner's expanded d2boot.log + `d2exe.map` finally pinned the freeze. hangwatch dump: main thread parked at **rva 0x5f059 = inside `D2Client::GAME::Roster::ROSTER_GroupPlayersIntoParties()`** (map base 0x400000; the `+0x49xxxx` offsets hangwatch printed are a base mismatch — runtime 0x890000 vs map 0x400000 — its nearest-symbol labels were wrong, the RVAs are ground truth). Resolved call chain: `MessageGameLoop → pfModes_EnterGame → Client_CheckConnectionTimeouts → NET_D2GS_CLIENT_HandleAllNonVariableSizePackets → NET_D2GS_CLIENT_PacketHandle_to0xAF (+0x30b) → NET_D2GS_CLIENT_Incoming0x5B (+0xc7) → ROSTER_AllocOrUpdatePlayer (+0x1b1) → ROSTER_RebuildPlayerParty (+0x55) → ROSTER_GroupPlayersIntoParties (+0x36, spin)` — the client was processing the s2c **0x5B player-join** packet (arrives after 0x04 STARTACT, so the trace's "during load" logging had gone silent). Root cause analysis: the party-regroup `do{...}while(true)` and its inlined collect loop provably terminate on well-formed chains (verified by a host harness replaying 50k randomized 0x5B/0x5C/0x75/0x8D sequences: zero spins/cycles/duplicates), so the chain must have been **cyclic from heap corruption upstream** — and the module contains four unbounded string copies that all end at `D2RosterStrc::pNext` (+0x80): `ROSTER_InitializeFields` name→+0x00, tag→+0x46, guild name→+0x4A, `sprintf` display name→+0x66, plus an unbounded `strlen` in the 0x5B handler and unbounded guild-name copies server-side (`Clients::GetGuildName/SetGuildName`). **Fix (5 files, overlay): (1) `ROSTER_SanitizePlayerChain()` — walk `gpRosterPlayers` with a 64-node cap, cut cycles, log `roster heal:` breadcrumb — called at the four walk entry points (GetRosterPlayerByGUID / GetRosterByGuidOrName / RebuildPlayerParty / PlayerLeft); (2) all `ROSTER_InitializeFields` copies bounded (valid names/tags byte-identical); (3) 0x5B handler bounds the guild scan to the packet's own length word (≤0x122) + logs `s2c 0x5B playerjoin guid= party= name=`; (4) server guild-name getter/setter capped.** The freeze becomes impossible; if the log shows `roster heal:` the upstream corruptor is still alive and the breadcrumb (with node count) plus the 0x5B marks narrow it next round. Harness + fuzzer in sandbox `scripts/roster_harness.cpp` / `roster_fuzz.cpp` (heal-on-cycle test passes; sanitizer verified no-op on clean chains). Gates: 11 new "d2boot-fix:" rows, suite **162/162**. Overlay zip delivered. — Rev 34 (2026-10-31, loading-freeze ROOT-CAUSE + FIX round): the owner's expanded d2boot.log came back with a hangwatch dump — main thread stuck INSIDE S→C packet dispatch (0x5B → roster) re-entering handlers forever, not idle. Root cause then hypothesized: cross-thread packet-queue race — the SP loopback enqueue (`HandleIncomingBufferAndCreatePacket`, server/qserver thread) mutated `pD2PacketClient_to_0xAE`/`_from_0xAF` with no lock while the main-thread pump popped them under `lpCriticalSection_00882ce8`; one torn interleave cycles the chain and the pump never drains (no main-loop beat, frozen loading screen). Fixed in 4 files (enqueue serialized; variable-size `default:` returns 0 instead of success-with-size-0; zero-consumption dispatch guard; pump drain watchdog), 4 new "d2boot-fix:" gates, suite 151/151. Overlay zip delivered. **Rev 35 note: the race fix stays in (correct hardening) but was NOT the freeze — the hangwatch dump shows the pump actively dispatching, spinning inside the 0x5B roster path, not blocked on the lock.** — Rev 33 (2026-10-31, loading-freeze diagnostics round): merge+vanilla-plus port compiles, boots, title screen + char select fine, single-player freezes at the end of the loading screen before entering the world. Owner directive: **diagnose from current code + d2boot.log only — no git-history archaeology** ("dont look at history. look at current code"). Owner deliverable preference: **overlay zips of changed files, not .patch files**. This round expands d2boot.log along the whole SP enter-world handshake (8 files, 36 anchored edits, gates 147/147) so one more owner run pinpoints the stage; reading guide + triage table in §8. Rev 32 (2026-10-30, gold-dialog round): owner field-reported the stash "deposit gold" panel misaligned at 720p — the modal gold-transaction dialog (deposit/withdraw, stash/inventory/cube entries, `ui::UI_ShowGoldTransactionDialog`) anchored its background at raw 800-space (215,140) with all four sub-widgets (spinner 0xdf,0xdb; edit box 0x102,0xe4; OK 0xfa,0x11f; cancel 0x163,0x11f) in absolute 800-space too, so it sat one height-delta too high and one width-delta too left. Fixed at the construction site: dialog origin + sub-widget coords ride `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()/Y()` (X: keeps vanilla's screen-centered placement, 400→640; Y: bottom-ride, 140→260). Draw and hit-test read the same stored coords, so both shift together — no inverse form needed. 0 at 800×600. Gates: ui.cpp Y 6→11 + new "gold dialog X shifts" x5 (suite 130/130). **Owner field-confirmed the same day: "Great job. Deposit Gold is aligned now."** Sibling dialogs still at raw 800-space (trade gold prompts, cube UI dialog, NPC menus, EscMenu) — exercise in §6 and ride any that misalign. Rev 31 (2026-10-30, renderer round): owner field-tested the providers at 720p — **Direct3D fullscreen (renderer 6) WORKS** ("-w is renderer 1, fullscreen is 6 so D3D does work"); the fps overlay's "Video: Software" line is the honest name of the windowed GDI provider (renderer 1). **Glide (renderer 4) fixed the same day**: `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitWindow` clamped every non-640 stop to an 800x600 surface AND left `gbGlidePerspectiveMode` 0 at a non-800x600 size — where `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitTextureMemory`'s non-perspective branch is gated on width==800 && height==600 and never runs, so the caches stay uninitialized (the D3D black-screen trap). Now: size table mode 4 = 1280x720 / mode 3 = dormant 1344x700, grSstWinOpen resolution enums 16/17, modes 2/3/4 take the perspective=1 path; 4 "resfix: glide*" gates (suite 129/129). **Requires the paired Glide3xVK wrapper patch** (`glide3xvk-720p-resolutions.patch` → `g3x_resolution_size` entries 16/17 + unknown-threshold 17) applied on branch `Glide3xVK`; older wrapper builds fail soft (640x480 + log line). Rev 30 (site-6 field-confirmed): belt potions and the inventory panel are correct (draws AND clicks; owner: "Great job. Belt Potions and Inventory Panel are correct"). Rev 29: the vertical half of the shift (§4) coded — `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` plus ~225 Y-ride sites across 9 files, gates "site-6:" (17 rows). Rev 28 history: handoff refocused on 720p, pre-720p content (revs 14–27, §1–§13) moved verbatim to HISTORY **H24**. Engineering record: [`docs/resolution-widelayout-audit.md`](docs/resolution-widelayout-audit.md); gates: `tools/check-vanilla-plus.sh` ("site-5:" + "site-6:" + "resfix:" rows).*

## 1. What this branch is

The `720p` branch adds a fourth stop to the in-game VIDEO OPTIONS Resolution row — **Vanilla Plus 1280x720** (resolution mode 4) — and renders the retail 800x600 UI class on it. The UI is *not* widened or relaid out: the exactly-800-px-wide retail interface (800CtrlPnl7 bar art, side panels, 800BorderFrame, and the `nScreenMode=1` D2Common layout tables inventory.txt / belts.txt) moves as ONE RIGID UNIT to the horizontal center of the playfield. The two fork-owned primitives (VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp}) are:

- `VPRES_IsWideLayoutMode(m)` — TRUE for modes 2 (retail 800x600), 3 (dormant 1344x700, same UI class) and 4 (1280x720).
- `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()` — `(ScreenSizeX - 800) / 2`, clamped ≥ 0. 0 at modes 0/1/2 (retail math byte-identical), **240 at 1280x720**, 272 at 1344x700.

`DRAW_UI` (UI/Draw.cpp :2064–2080) sets `gnPanelOffsetX = 80 + offset`, `gnPanelOffsetY = -60` per frame; bottom-anchored expressions (`gnPanelOffsetY - const + ScreenSizeY`, bar pieces at `ScreenSizeY`) follow the screen height automatically. DC6 draws extend **upward** from the y argument (Rev 19 postmortem in H20), so bar art anchored at `ScreenSizeY` renders bottom-of-screen at any height.

## 4. Live defect: the vertical half of the shift (root-caused in-tree)

Site 5's mechanism re-centers X only. Two Y anchoring styles coexist in the 800-class code, and they disagree at 720p:

- **Bottom-anchored** (`gnPanelOffsetY - const + ScreenSizeY`, bar/orb art at `ScreenSizeY`): self-adapts — everything shifts down by `ScreenSizeY - 600` (+120 at 720p). No action needed.
- **800-absolute table Y and top-anchored raw constants**: stay at their 800x600 values → land exactly one height-delta too high.

CONFIRMED — belt (the floating potions): every Belts.txt consumer in Panel.cpp shifts X and leaves Y raw. Item draw `DrawItemGfx(pItem, aRectItem[0] + offset, aRectItem[2])` (:2045), green/red slot highlights (:2024, :2041, helper :789), hotkey text (:2071), and the belt hit test `PANEL_GetPanelLayoutHeight` (:1471–1494 — Y pair never offset, so clicks target the phantom 800-space slots too). Bar art sits at 624..720; belt rects stay at ~545..604.

CONFIRMED — inventory: `VP_SHIFT_InvPageCoords / InvGridCoords / InvBodyCoords` (Inv.cpp :140–156) shift X bounds only; the panel art is bottom-anchored (4-tile `gnPanelOffsetY - 0xe0/-0x30 + ScreenSizeY` pattern, e.g. ui.cpp :5045–5054) and moves +120 → grid/body/page rects sit 120 px above their art. Same for hireling / trade / bank / cube / stash / npc-shop page+grid coords fetched in `InitializeiInventoryPages` (:159–286).

SAME CLASS, needs the same offset — top-anchored raw-constant sites (eyeball to confirm): 800BorderFrame pieces at raw Y `0x3f/0xfd/0x1e4/0x229` (Panel.cpp :1435–1443, :1460–1468); hireling name/stat texts (`0xd6 - gnPanelOffsetY` :5618, label rows :5729–5758, stat rows :5873–5875); trade window texts (`0x1b/0xf2 - gnPanelOffsetY` :9230–9276); stash gold text (:8881–8886 — **confirmed bottom-anchored on eyeball, `gnPanelOffsetY - 0x19f + ScreenSizeY`, no fix needed**); weapon-swap tooltip (`0x15 - gnPanelOffsetY` :8384); recipe scroll text (`0x82 - gnPanelOffsetY` :6727).

## 5. Fix round (site-6) — what landed

1. **Primitive**: `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()` in VanillaPlusCore.{h,cpp} — `ScreenSizeY - 600` clamped ≥ 0: 0 at modes 0/1/2 (byte-identical retail), +100 at mode 3, +120 at mode 4. `gnPanelOffsetY` untouched (-60) — bottom-anchored expressions already adapt; double-shifting is the failure mode. Design per audit doc default: panels keep retail's bottom margin (hug the bar).
2. **Table rects**: `VP_SHIFT_InvPageCoords / GridCoords / BodyCoords` now shift `nTop`/`nBottom` too (covers inventory + hireling + trade + bank + cube + npc-shop + trophy + guild-vault pages). Every Belts.txt consumer in Panel.cpp shifts the Y pair: slot highlights (:789/:2027/:2044), item sprite (:2050), hotkey text (:2076), and the hit test (`nTop/nBottom` in `PANEL_GetPanelLayoutHeight` — clicks followed the draw).
3. **Top-anchored constants**: 800BorderFrame raw-Y pieces (10 draws); hireling name/stat rows; trade window texts; weapon-swap tooltip; cube recipe scroll; party panel art + member rows + all click rects; waypoint act tabs/list/rows + the help-bubble; quest log icons/titles/description + open/close buttons; help screen (Xpac border/bullets/text rows — classic help untouched, mirroring site-5's Xpac-only X); stat-window tooltip hit tests; Engine modal popups (OK/cancel art, text, and their hit-test bases). Scrolling text box panel-mode top. Left alone deliberately: repair icon + help scroll button + hireling/pet onscreen icons (ScreenSize-anchored corner widgets) and the classic-help path.
4. **Input half**: where the code converts screen→panel-relative mouse (`nMouseY += gnPanelOffsetY` idiom — waypoint :462/:684/:750, quest log :1794/:1848/:2072) the shift is subtracted back out: `+= gnPanelOffsetY - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetY()`. Site-5 already did the X twin (`-= gnPanelOffsetX`) here.
5. **Trap fixed — coord cache key**: `nCoordPageKey` was `nScreenMode + 2·(wide-X)` which collides for mode 3 (272/100) and mode 4 (240/120) — now `nScreenMode + X + 0x400·Y` (raw offsets, injective for all 12 combos), so an in-game 3↔4 switch refetches coords.
6. **Gates**: 17 "site-6:" rows (primitive, per-file shift counts, waypoint/questlog input-inverse counts, 4 new fork-api includes); suite 125/125. `fork-surface.allowlist` must be regenerated by the owner after committing (`tools/check-fork-surface.sh --update`) — the sandbox has no `upstream` ref.

**Regression surface**: every offset is 0 at modes 0/1/2, so 800x600 must stay byte-identical and 640x480 untouched — the §6 checklist applies to 800x600 as well as 720p.

## 8. Current task (rev 34): SP loading-screen freeze — ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED

**Task (owner, verbatim)**: 'merge+vanilla-plus port compiles, boots, goes to title screen, char select. freezes at end of loading screen. Expanding d2boot.log to diagnose'

**The rev-34 owner run delivered the answer.** The expanded d2boot.log shows the whole handshake completing — `s2c 0x04 STARTACT received - loading screen can drop now` — followed 12.9 s later by a **hangwatch stall dump** (`no main-loop beat for 12906 ms`). The dumped main-thread stack (d2exe.map symbolization; small offsets reliable):

```
#7  MessageGameLoop
#5/#6  S->C packet pump (Game.cpp HandleAllNonVariableSizePackets -> PacketHandle_to0xAF)
#4  NET_D2GS_CLIENT_PacketHandle_to0xAF +0x30B
#3  NET_D2GS_CLIENT_Incoming0x5B +0xC7        <- player-join/roster packet handler
#2  ROSTER_AllocOrUpdatePlayer +0x1B1
#0/#1 roster list code (GetRosterName map blob) <- eip spinning here
```

Reading: the main thread was NOT blocked/idle — it was stuck INSIDE S→C packet dispatch, processing packet 0x5B (player join → `ROSTER_AllocOrUpdatePlayer`) over and over. Every roster path was audited in-tree and terminates for a single fresh player (AllocOrUpdate/InitializeFields/RebuildPlayerParty/GroupIntoParties/SortAllParties/Flatten/ResetPartyUi all bounded with one roster node); the loop is NOT in the roster code — the dispatch machinery was re-delivering packets forever.

**Root cause (rev 34): cross-thread packet-queue race.** `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_ExtractPacketSize` (D2Net/SRC/D2Net.cpp) pops S→C packets off `pD2PacketClient_to_0xAE`/`pD2PacketClient_from_0xAF` **under `lpCriticalSection_00882ce8`** (main thread, every frame). In SP the server is in-process: `NET_D2GS_SERVER_SendPacketToClient` loops server packets straight back through `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_HandleIncomingBufferAndCreatePacket` (D2Net/SRC/Client.cpp) — **on the server/qserver thread, mutating the same two queues with NO lock**. The enqueue walks the chain to the tail and writes the head back while the pop can free nodes under it; one bad interleave leaves `pD2PacketClientNext` pointing at freed/recycled nodes — a self-sustaining cycle. The pump (`do { ExtractPacketSize; dispatch; } while (iSize != -1);`) then never sees -1, `MessageGameLoop` never returns, the main-loop beat stops (the hangwatch dump), and the loading screen — which drops only when the frame loop progresses past 0x04 — freezes. The race window is widest exactly at the post-0x04 packet burst (0x5B join + pets + quest state), which is why the hang lands at the END of the loading screen. Retail D2 ran the SP server on the main thread (unlocked queue was safe there); the port's threaded qserver turned it into a data race.

**Fixes (rev 34, 4 files)**:
1. `D2Net/SRC/Client.cpp` — `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_HandleIncomingBufferAndCreatePacket` now takes `lpCriticalSection_00882ce8` around the whole enqueue and releases it on every exit path. **The root-cause fix** (matches the lock discipline the pop side always had).
2. `D2Net/Game/Server.cpp` — `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_GetIncomingPacketSizeFromTableAndVariableSize`: the variable-size switch `default:` now returns **0 (unknown size)** instead of `1` with `*pExpectedSize = 0`. Success-with-size-0 made the dispatcher re-dispatch one packet forever (cursor never advances) and the TCP parser enqueue zero-size nodes forever — latent poison for any future variable-length opcode (all 14 current ones have explicit cases, so this is hardening, verified against `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_INCOMING_SIZE`).
3. `D2Client/GAME/SCmd.cpp` — `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_PacketHandle_to0xAF` zero-consumption guard: a dispatched packet that consumes 0 bytes logs `s2c dispatch guard: op 0x.. consumed 0 bytes; dropping N trailing bytes` and stops processing that buffer instead of spinning forever.
4. `D2Client/GAME/Game/Game.cpp` — pump drain watchdog: every 4096 packets drained within one pass logs `s2c pump: drained %u packets (op<0xAF|op>=0xAF) in one pass`, so a runaway drain is now visible in d2boot.log instead of being a silent infinite loop.

Gates: 4 new "d2boot-fix:" rows (suite **151/151**). Delivered as an **overlay zip of changed files** (owner preference; includes the still-uncommitted rev-33 instrumentation files so the zip is self-contained over a clean tree). After committing, regenerate `fork-surface.allowlist` owner-side (`tools/check-fork-surface.sh --update` — the sandbox has no `upstream` ref).

**Expected healthy tail for the next owner run** — after `s2c 0x04 STARTACT received - loading screen can drop now` the world should simply appear; the rev-34 lines only fire on pathology (`s2c dispatch guard: ...` = size-table gap; repeating `s2c pump: drained N packets ...` = queue never drains; plus a hangwatch dump naming the function if anything still wedges).

---

### Rev 33 (diagnostics round, retained)


**Task**: merge+vanilla-plus port compiles, boots, title screen and char select work; single-player freezes at the end of the loading screen, before the world appears. **Constraint (owner directive)**: diagnose from the *current* tree + the owner-provided `d2boot.log` only — no regression archaeology in commit history.

**Evidence so far** (`d2boot.log`, owner run): every stage up to `enter-world: game loop enter` succeeds (MPQs, GFX provider, window, sound, qserver start, connect wait `connected=1`, game data init). Then silence. No hangwatch dump before the owner killed the process — so either the main thread kept beating (a *live-loop* stall, not a wedged wait) or the kill came inside the 12 s threshold. `Client_CheckConnectionTimeouts` early-returns for SP (Game.cpp :2305), so nothing will ever time this load out — the hang is genuinely infinite, not a slow load.

**Mechanism established from current code (rev 33)**: in SP the "server" is cooperative — it is pumped from the *client's* frame function (`CLIENT_GameLoopFrame`, Game.cpp :2419–2431): `NET_D2GS_SERVER_HandleAnyIncomingPacket` → `SrvProcessAllGames(1)` (25 FPS gate) → `QSERVER_DispatchAndCleanup` (flushes S→C packets back into the same process). The loading screen drops only when the client handles S→C **0x04 STARTACT** (`NET_D2GS_CLIENT_Incoming0x04_STARTACT` sets `ServerLoadingComplete=1`, SCmd.cpp :422). The whole handshake:

1. EnterGame sends c2s **0x67** create-game (Game.cpp :2709, Msg.cpp :42).
2. `CCmd` case 'g' → `SetupGameChecks` gate → `GAME_CreateSingleplayerGame`: allocates game + client, queues S→C 0x01 NEWGAME + state-cmd **0** + state-cmd **2**, sets client `ConnectionState=1`.
3. Client 0x02 handler (`NET_D2GS_CLIENT_Incoming0x02_LoadSuccess`) replies c2s **0x6B** join-act.
4. `CCmd` case 'k' → `NET_IsClientAllowedToJoin` (requires `ConnectionState==1`) → `NET_D2GS_SERVER_SrvJoinAct`: `CLIENT_LoadCharacterAndSendGameData` (save load) → `CLIENT_LoadActAndSetState2` (`EnsureDrlgActLoaded` **DRLG worldgen** + `TellClientToLoadAct` queues 0x03 NEWACT + 0x53) → `CLIENT_PlacePlayerInRoom` → `ConnectionState=3`.
5. `UpdateClients` case 3 waits `DRLGROOM_AreAllNearRoomsActive(pClient->pRoom)`, then queues **0x04** → client sets `ServerLoadingComplete=1` → loading screen can drop.

**Instrumentation added (rev 33)** — all `[d2boot]`-tagged, all quiet once 0x04 arrives: client frame telemetry (`game-loop: ...` while loading), one line per distinct S→C opcode during load (`s2c recv 0x..`), explicit 0x02/0x04 handler marks, c2s 0x67/0x6B send marks, bounded server dequeue trace (`srv c2s ...`), SP create enter/exit, `srv state-cmd` choke-point log, `SrvJoinAct` stage marks (enter / char-load refusal / char ok / placed), `join: load-act` + `DRLG: generating act`/`act generated` worldgen marks, `client state N -> M` transition log, state-3 `waiting for rooms` telemetry (first + every 250 server frames), `join: all rooms active -> 0x04` mark, server frame marks (first tick + every 250 up to frame 1000). Files: D2Client `GAME/Game/Game.cpp`, `GAME/SCmd.cpp`, `GAME/Msg.cpp`; D2Game `GAME/Server.cpp`, `GAME/CCmd.cpp`, `GAME/Clients.cpp`, `GAME/SCmd.cpp`, `GAME/Level.cpp`. Plus 16 `d2boot:` gate rows (suite 147/147). Delivered as an **overlay zip** (owner applies over their tree, builds via `tools/build-local-win11.cmd`); after committing, regenerate `fork-surface.allowlist` owner-side (`tools/check-fork-surface.sh --update`).

**Healthy-load narrative to expect** (timestamps in ms; `[JOIN n]` SRVLog lines go to the server log, not here):

```
enter-world: game loop enter
game-loop: first frame after 'game loop enter' (still loading)
c2s send 0x67 create-game (gameType=3 class=.. diff=..)
srv c2s setup pkt (clientid .., op 0x67)
c2s 0x67 GAMELOGON_SP recv (clientid ..)
SP create-game enter (clientid .. class .. diff ..)
srv state-cmd 0x0 queued (clientid ..)
SP create-game done (0x01 + state-cmd 0 + state-cmd 2 queued)
s2c recv 0x01 during load / s2c recv 0x00 during load / s2c recv 0x02 during load
s2c 0x02 LoadSuccess -> replying c2s 0x6B join-act
c2s send 0x6B join-act
srv c2s setup pkt (clientid .., op 0x6b)
c2s 0x6B JOINGAME recv (clientid .., allowed=1)
SrvJoinAct enter (clientid ..)
join: LoadCharacter enter (clientid .., fromSave 1)
SrvJoinAct: character loaded ok
join: load-act enter (act 0)
DRLG: generating act 0 (first load)
DRLG: act generated
join: DRLG act ready
join: TellClientToLoadAct (act 0)
join: 0x03 NEWACT + 0x53 ACTTIMEOFDAY queued, state -> 2
client state 1 -> 2 (clientid ..)
SrvJoinAct: player placed in room, state -> 3 (waiting rooms)
client state 2 -> 3 (clientid ..)
server: first game tick (world loop running)
server: game frame 250/500/750/1000
join: all rooms active -> 0x04 STARTACT queued (loading done server-side)
srv state-cmd 0x4 queued (clientid ..)
s2c recv 0x04 during load
s2c 0x04 STARTACT received - loading screen can drop now
```

**Triage table — read the LAST d2boot line, it names the broken stage**:

| last line seen | broken stage / where to look next |
| --- | --- |
| `enter-world: game loop enter`, no `game-loop: first frame` | `MessageGameLoop` never ran the frame fn — cutscene state stuck (`D2GFX_EndCutSceneReturnSceneState` at CLIENT_GameLoopFrame top) |
| first frame ok, no `c2s send 0x67` | EnterGame's send branch didn't run (gametype routing before the loop) |
| 0x67 sent, no `srv c2s setup pkt` | client→server loopback dead: Fog QServer mode-0 queue / packet-size classifier |
| setup pkt seen, no `SP create-game enter` | `SetupGameChecks` rejected — the `c2s 0x67 REJECTED` line names it |
| create enter, no `create done` | creation hung/crashed inside (subsystem allocs, seed, quest/monster/object controls) + hangwatch dump |
| create done, no `s2c recv 0x02` | S→C dispatch dead: `QSERVER_DispatchAndCleanup`/`FlushClientPackets` loopback |
| 0x02 recv, no `c2s send 0x6B` | client 0x02 handler path (Msg.cpp `NET_D2GS_CLIENT_Send_0x6B`) |
| `0x6B JOINGAME recv (allowed=0)` | `ConnectionState != 1` at 0x6B time — ordering/double-send; also `QSERVER_IsClientAllowedToJoin` |
| `SrvJoinAct enter`, no `character loaded ok` | save load hung, or REFUSED line (0xB4 reason) — `CLIENT_LoadOrCreatePlayerUnit` |
| `load-act enter`, no `DRLG: act generated` | worldgen hang — hangwatch dump names the DRLG function |
| act generated, no `player placed in room` | `CLIENT_PlacePlayerInRoom` hang (corpse/golem restore paths) |
| `STILL waiting for rooms` repeating | `DRLGROOM_AreAllNearRoomsActive` never true — room activation (`InitNewRooms`, act dirty flags) |
| 0x04 queued, no `s2c recv 0x04` | server→client loopback dead (same class as row 6, opposite direction) |
| `s2c 0x04 STARTACT received` but screen frozen | client-side dismissal path — loading draw fn / load timer (`gLoadTimerDurationMs`), Game.cpp :2668–2683 |

If the log ends mid-narrative AND a `hangwatch: === stall detected` block appears, the dump's symbolized stack IS the answer (copy `d2exe.map` beside d2.exe for names). If the loop stays alive with no further marks, the table's "no <mark>" row is the diagnosis. Next round: one owner run with this build, paste the new `d2boot.log` tail.

### Rev journal, revs 40–56 (verbatim)

*Rev 56 (2026-10-31, track C skeleton re-root-cause + second fix round — **the R50 fix was necessary but NOT sufficient: the spawn-time write carried the same inverted polarity**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R50` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R51`**): owner: **"Necro Skeletons are still yellow"** — the R50 build field-tested, symptom persists (issue #8 stays open; body = video only, no comments). **Rev 55's documented fallback family evaluated FIRST and EXONERATED**: the "dat read base off-by-4 rotation" cannot swap the white/yellow tables — a `+4`-vs-`+0` delta shifts every 256-byte palette by 4 entries (first 4 slots subtly wrong) but keeps the table ORDER intact; the loader layout is self-consistent (per-monster entries `[4-byte monsterId][transforms...]`, free-list next-ptr at +0x1004 = 4 + 4096; `RandTransforms.dat` is flat 30 × 0x100, no header) and the field datum from R49+R50 (spawn-written idx 1 renders YELLOW over retail MPQ bytes) pins the pair: idx 1 = yellow, idx 0 = bone-white. **Real root cause — the SECOND write site**: `CreateObject` (`D2Client/UNIT/CUnit.cpp:2249-2260`, the packet-created-unit path — called from the server-command dispatcher `SCmd.cpp:523`, so every client-visible monster in SP too) has the MonStats2 `Utrans==0xFF` ownership fallback transcribed as `nPalShiftIdx = (uint32_t)!uMercType` — the SAME inverted polarity R50 fixed in the roster recolors, at the site that runs FIRST. A raised skeleton spawns straight into idx 1 (yellow hostile table); `ROSTER_UpdateHostileColors`/`ROSTER_UpdateMercHostileColors` are event-driven (roster-update packet handler `Roster.cpp:1763`, merc packet `Roster.cpp:1457`) and never fire for SP summons between spawn and first draw — the wrong tint stuck for the unit's whole life. Explains BOTH field reports: R49 (spawn 1 + roster 1) and R50 (spawn 1 + roster 0-never-fires) both rendered yellow. Provenance: the `!uMercType` is verbatim at `44e3518` (baseline `CUnit.cpp:2215`) — **baseline transcription defect, not a merge casualty**; repo-wide grep confirms it was the LAST inverted hostility→palshift write in the tree (the only other `!bHostile` hit is the R50 comment text). Honest correction to the Rev 55 mechanism chain: step (2) mapped the unique roll and the superunique `Utrans` override but MISSED the `Utrans==0xFF` ownership branch sitting in the same block. **Fix (R51)**: spawn writes the RAW flag — `nPalShiftIdx = (uint32_t)uMercType` + [R51] invariant comment; both write sites (spawn + roster recolors) now agree on the raw-flag convention (own/ally = 0 → bone-white sub-table 0, hostile owner = 1 → yellow sub-table 1), and the latch stays consistent (spawn writes `GFX_SetCofDataFlags` + `GFX_SetPalShiftIndex` from the same variable). **Gate row `spawn: ownership palshift polarity (R51)`** (awk over CUnit.cpp: inverted=0 / raw=1; negative-tested against the pre-fix file: inverted=1 raw=0 → FAIL). Version gates re-pinned R50→R51 (banner + build version). Suite: **205/205**. Audits re-run: placement (OK=48/MOVED=139/NEW=153/GONE=2, unchanged), wrong-branch (60 checked, 0 hits). **Field-verify asks**: rebuild (R51 — source round), first d2boot.log mark must read `Vanilla+0.0.6-R51`; (1) raise skeletons → bone-white AT SPAWN (no roster event involved); (2) MP regression: hostile-owner summons now spawn yellow too (pre-fix they spawned white — the tell was inverted on BOTH sites); (3) R49 unique/champion tints + R50 roster recolor unchanged; (4) 800x600 + act bosses unchanged. **If own skeletons are STILL yellow on R51**, the remaining suspect family is the palshift.dat entry parse itself (loader table order vs retail) — discriminator: force idx 2 (TransLvl path) on a skeleton and compare its render against the idx 0/1 renders. Overlay: `vanilla-plus-0.0.6-R51-skeleton-spawn-polarity.zip`.

*Rev 55 (2026-10-31, track C skeleton root-cause + fix round — **minion skeletons yellow/gold ROOT-CAUGHT and FIXED**, self-ID bumped **`Vanilla+0.0.6-R49` → `Vanilla+0.0.6-R50`**): owner: **"Minion Skeletons are still Gold thou. They should be more white."** + pointed at **[issue #8](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/8)** ("[Video] Minion Skeletons are Yellow.", opened 2026-10-29 by the owner, body = video, no comments — predates R49, so the symptom rode the 44e3518-era build too). **Provenance first (house rule)**: `ROSTER_UpdateHostileColors`/`ROSTER_UpdateMercHostileColors` and the whole `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` skeleton chain are IDENTICAL `44e3518` → main modulo regen churn — **a BASELINE transcription defect, not a merge casualty** (same class as R49; no marker audit could ever catch it). Every upstream branch (jaenster-main, PR1-5, 720p) carries the same code — no fix to cherry-pick. **Mechanism chain (fully mapped this round)**: (1) local spawn init (`Monster.cpp:1297`) sets `palshift = GfxSequenceData = TransLvl+2` for every created monster — skeletons (no Trans) land on idx 2; (2) packet-created units (`CUnit.cpp:2237/2273-2274`) roll via `PALSHIFT_GenerateShiftDuration` (unique tints 9-29, R49's path), override superuniques with `Utrans[difficulty]`, clamp ≥0x1e → 2, and write BOTH `GFX_SetCofDataFlags` and `GFX_SetPalShiftIndex`; (3) `CUnit::SetGfxSequenceData` IS `GFX_SetCofDataFlags` (`CUnit.cpp:144-158`) — dwCofDataFlags is the roster re-sync LATCH SHADOW: `ROSTER_UpdateMercHostileColors` only rewrites palshift while it still equals the shadow (nobody touched palshift alone); (4) the roster pet-recolor (`ROSTER_UpdateHostileColors` for nSkillID==4 pets + `ROSTER_UpdateMercHostileColors` for the 0x16b/0x16c skeleton classes per owner) then writes the hostility value into both fields; (5) draw time (`PalShift.cpp:295`): idx 0/1 passes the case 0/1 class check ONLY for 0x16b/0x16c and resolves through the bottom return `entry + idx*0x100 + 4` = the monster's own palshift.dat sub-table. **Root cause**: the four recolor writes use `(uint32_t)!bHostile` — the INVERTED polarity. `ROSTER_AreUnitsHostile` is sane (own pet → `return 0`, hostile/unknown → 1), the palshift.dat bytes are retail MPQ data, and retail own skeletons are bone-white — so retail must write the RAW flag: bHostile=0 (own/ally) → sub-table 0 (bone-white), bHostile=1 (hostile owner) → sub-table 1 (yellow tell). The `!` fed every own raised skeleton the yellow HOSTILE table — exactly issue #8's video. Green-blood path exonerated: `PALSHIFT_ApplyGreenBloodPalette` writes only the [0x804..0x1004) half and early-outs for skeletons (byLocalBlood != 2) — the file half is untouched. Also resolved the Rev 53 ask #4 by mechanism: the yellow population is ROSTER PETS (necro summons; unique-pack minion skeletons ride the static-init idx 2 path and should have been white all along — owner can confirm both in the field pass). **Fix (R50)**: the four writes flipped to `(uint32_t)bHostile` with [R50] invariant comments (both fields per site stay equal-valued — latch preserved). **Gate row `roster: pet recolor polarity (R50)`** (awk: inverted=0 / raw=4 across the SetPalShiftIndex+SetGfxSequenceData recolor lines; negative-tested against the pre-fix file: inverted=4 raw=0 → FAIL). Version gates re-pinned R49→R50 (banner + build version). Suite: **204/204**. (2) `MONSTER_RollComponentAppearance` (`Monster.cpp:346`) rolls `seed % aComponents[i]` per component into the unit-data bytes (the `szCharName + 4` union alias) inside the generic spawn init `MONSTER_InitStats` ← `STRUCT_FillDataMonster`, plus the region appearance-set path for champion packs; (3) the 0xAC wire is protocol-correct both ends — the server writer and the client parser derive per-component bit widths from the same `aComponents` values (which PROVES `aComponents` is a count, not a fixed index: a fixed index v≥4 is unrepresentable in `bits(v-1)`), so the proposal's memcpy would write counts into index fields and produce out-of-range variants; (4) the client copies the packet payload into unit data (`CUnit.cpp:2214-2222`) and the composite reads it (`GFXUTIL_GetCompositePaletteIndex` → `MONSTER_GetComponentOverlay` bounds-checks against `aComponents` and indexes `aCompCodes`). Function-level pre-port provenance (`scripts/rev49_trackc_provenance.sh`, kept): all seven chain functions IDENTICAL `44e3518` → main modulo regen churn — **not a merge casualty; the owner's "no code copies components" symptom-reading was right about the observable, wrong about the site.** **Real root cause — `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` (`D2Client/GAME/PalShift.cpp:295`)**: the transcribed body early-returns `nullptr` when the monster has no `palshift.dat` entry (`gpMonsterPalShiftList[classId]` empty) — BEFORE the switch — but `RandTransforms.dat` is a GLOBAL table (0x1e00 bytes = 30 × 0x100, loaded once at init, `gbPalShiftRandTransformLoaded`), and the rolled unique/champion tint indices 9-29 (the client creation roll: `PALSHIFT_GenerateShiftDuration` = `seed % 0x1e + 9`, clamped ≥0x1e → 2) resolve EXCLUSIVELY through that global table — the entry pointer is consumed only by the index 0-7 sub-table paths (blood palettes + the 4 hardcoded palshift.dat classes 0x16b/0x16c/0x1a4/0x1a5). Net effect: every random unique rolled a tint index at creation and then looked it up through a dead path — `pPalShift` null → `D2GFX_DrawImage` null remap → base palette. **Exactly "Unique Mobs don't have Unique Colors", invisible to every presence-based audit since the baseline transcription (branch history: PalShift.cpp never touched post-Ghidra).** External confirmation: RandTransforms.dat holds the champion/unique/superunique color palettes (30 usable of 37 since the 1.10 skip-last-7 change). **Fix (R49)**: the unconditional entry-out DELETED; behavior-identical guards moved into the two entry-consuming arms (case 0/1 after the class check, case 2-7 head) — every currently-working path byte-same, the 8-0x25 path now resolves for any monster. **Gate row `palshift: rand-transform reachable (R49)`** (awk: entry-guards must all sit AFTER the `switch (nPalShiftIndex)` line, exactly 2, and the RandTransform return must exist — negative-tested against the pre-fix file: switch=308 guards=0 early=1). Suite: **203/203**. ## 3. Defect track A — Sound silent at/after title screen — **CLOSED (field-verified rev42)**

Kept as the record of the defect class (IEEE-754 bit-pattern mistranscription) and the fix spec; §9.1 hunts the same class elsewhere.

### 3.0 The two audio paths (why intro works and everything after doesn't)

1. **Intro video (Bink)**: `BinkSetSoundSystem(BinkOpenDirectSound, 0)` (`D2Client/D2Client.cpp:4033`) — Bink opens its **own** DirectSound instance, independent of the game engine. Field-confirmed working.
2. **Game engine**: `SOUND_Initialize` at boot (`D2Client/Engine/Application.cpp:170–179`, real-hwnd fix already in) → `D2Sound/Sound.cpp` up to 3× `SOUND_InitDirectSound` attempts (EAX/3D downgrades) → on success: primary buffer play, `SOUNDSMP_CreateSoundThread`, `SOUND_InitMusicBuffer` (`SFileDdaInitialize` — the DDA music stream), `SOUNDSMP_SetGlobalVolume(0xff)`. Title music = DDA stream (`D2SoundSmp` → `SFileDdaBeginEx`); UI/game SFX = the sfx channels (`D2Client/GAME/Sound.cpp`). **Every volume→attenuation conversion funnels through `SOUNDSMP_ClampVolume` / `SOUNDSMP_ClampAttenuation` (`D2Sound/Src/D2SoundSmp.cpp`).**

### 3.1 Prime suspect (verified in-tree this round): the merge dropped the defect-12 fix

- main's `D2Sound/Src/D2SoundSmp.cpp` carries the raw hex-literal casts:
  - `ClampVolume` epsilons: :769, :774 — `(long double)(double)0x3f1a36e2e0000000`
  - `ClampVolume` scale: :785 — `(long double)(double)-0x3f60c00000000000` (decodes to −0.00341796875; retail scale is −2000.0)
  - `ClampAttenuation`: :790 epsilon, :793 bottom bound `(double)-0x3f3c780000000000` (decodes to −0.0004344), :797 divisor `(double)0x409f400000000000` (retail: 2000.0)
- In C++ `0x3f1a36e2e0000000` is the **integer** ≈4.54e18 (Ghidra printed the double `0.0001` as an IEEE-754 bit pattern and the port transcribed it literally), so `value <= min + eps` fires on **every** input → every conversion returns −10000.0 → **every engine buffer pinned at DSBVOLUME_MIN**. The intro video is immune (separate path). Field signature matches defect 12 exactly.
- **Provenance (verified via git)**: `720p` HAS the decoded constants (8 sites); upstream `pr4/sound-except-intro` HAS them (PR #10 commit 2); main's last change to the file is `d6affdd "merge port"` — **the fix did not survive the merge**. First confirmed casualty of the owner's "fixes not re-litigated" caveat; precedent is H17 (ad964f3 superseded 6 D3D/glide hand fixes).
- Why no gate caught it: the 183-row gate suite covers site-5/6/7 UI+glide and `d2boot-fix:` rows but has **zero sound rows**. Add sound gate rows when the fix lands (§5 rule).

### 3.2 What DID survive the merge (do not redo)

- **fix 16** — `D2Client/GAME/Sound.cpp:523–539` reads `SOUNDSMP_CalculateVolumeAndPan` out params as integers (`[vanilla-plus fix 16]` comment in-tree; H3 Defect 13, field-verified "The sounds works now. NPC's Monsters, Player. All working.").
- **the hwnd fix** — `Application.cpp:170–179` passes the real window handle (`[sound fix]` comment in-tree).
- **d2boot sound marks** — `Application.cpp:175–177`: `SOUND_Initialize enter (hwnd=...)` / `SOUND_Initialize done (dsoundInit=%d eax=%d)`.

### 3.3 The fix (verbatim spec from H3 Defect 12 / upstream PR #10 commit 2) — **LANDED rev41, FIELD-VERIFIED rev42** (code identical to pr4-fixed; curve harness ALL PASS; gates 188/188; owner: "Sound works.")

In `D2Sound/Src/D2SoundSmp.cpp`, decode five literals and restore the scale — nothing else:

| line | now | becomes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| :769 | `(long double)(double)0x3f1a36e2e0000000` | `(long double)0.0001` |
| :774 | `(long double)(double)0x3f1a36e2e0000000` | `(long double)0.0001` |
| :785 | `(float)(fVar1 * (long double)(double)-0x3f60c00000000000)` | `(float)(fVar1 * -2000.0)` |
| :790 | `(long double)(double)0x3f1a36e2e0000000` | `(long double)0.0001` |
| :793 | `(long double)(double)-0x3f3c780000000000` | `(long double)-10000.0` |
| :797 | `(long double)(double)0x409f400000000000` | `(long double)2000.0` |

Curve of record (retail FUN_005165f0, user-supplied): `value ≤ min+0.0001 → −10000`; `value ≥ max−0.0001 → 0`; else `−2000·log10((max+min)/value)` centibels; `SOUNDSMP_ClampAttenuation` (10^(att/2000)) is its exact inverse. Verified: 255→0, 110→−730, 0→−10000. DirectSound: −10000 = absolute mute, 0 = full. Keep the comments self-contained (upstream-clean wording, the pr4 commit comments are the model). Overlay zip over main, CRLF byte-exact, `VP-BEGIN/VP-END` markers (upstream file), gates for the decoded tokens (§8).

### 3.4 Discriminator first — read d2boot.log before touching code

- `SOUND_Initialize done (dsoundInit=1 ...)` → engine alive → the ClampVolume pinning is fully consistent; land §3.3.
- `dsoundInit=0` → engine dead, different track: the game log then contains `DirectSound error: ...` lines (`SOUND_InitDirectSound` logs every failing call — DirectSoundCreate / SetCooperativeLevel / CreateSoundBuffer / GetCaps / SetFormat / EAX probe) naming the exact failing call; fix that instead.
- If after §3.3 music works but SFX stay silent → re-walk the fix-16 chain with the sounddiag marks **re-cut, not reinvented** (mark formats of record survive in HISTORY.md H4/sounddiag: `SetVolume#N: vol= glob= lin= att= buf= hr= from=`, `updchan:`, `calc:`, `fadein:`/`fadevol:`).

### 3.5 Do-not list

- **Do NOT re-audit the SMP layer** — H3 exoneration: halt IDs, struct offsets, float literals, master multiply, EAX property IDs all match retail ("Do not re-audit the SMP layer").
- **Do NOT touch `support/d2_loud.cpp` for sound issues** — it is the LOUD diagnostic build's bounds/guard/canary handlers, not audio (name trap).
- **Do NOT chase the SetPan right-branch negation** as a silence cause — carried watch item (H3): stereo position only.
- **Do NOT merge PR branches to "get the fix"** — owner: they are upstreamed; §3.3 above is the complete record.

## 4. Defect track B — Glide not booting — **ROOTED + FIXED + FIELD-VERIFIED R46 — CLOSED** (issue #11)

### 4.1 Architecture split (owner-corrected)

- **Game side (main)**: `D2Glide/` + `D2Client/Renderer/Glide.cpp` — loads glide3x.dll via `GetProcAddress("_grSstWinOpen@28", ...)` etc. Stays in main.
- **DLL side (branch `Glide3xVK`)**: the glide3x.dll wrapper — **all DLL work happens there**, including the resolution table.

### 4.2 Verified in-tree this round (main, game side)

- Site-7 intact: size table mode 4 = 1280x720 / mode 3 = dormant 1344x700 (`D2Glide/Renderer/Glide.cpp:132–133, 597–610`); `grSstWinOpen` resolution enums 16/17 (:605/:609); modes 2/3/4 take the perspective=1 init path (gates `resfix: glide*` green within 183/183).
- Wrapper contract on `Glide3xVK`: `src/g3x_types.h` `g3x_resolution_size` carries `{1280,720}`=16 and `{1344,700}`=17 **in-source** (the old `glide3xvk-720p-resolutions.patch` is absorbed — no standalone .patch file exists anymore); `src/glide_exports.cpp:130–131` honors enums ≤17. The soft-fail signature ("unknown resolution 16" in glide3x.log + 640x480 surface) belongs to wrapper builds **older** than that absorption.
- Zero renderer files changed between the "Glide works!" field confirmation (0.0.4 era, rev31/round-4, H25) and now — the failure mode this run is unknown (black screen? soft-fail? crash?).

### 4.3 Triage plan (next owner round)

1. Owner supplies: `glide3x.log` + `d2boot.log` from a `-3dfx` run, and which glide3x.dll build is deployed (date/provenance).
2. `unknown resolution 16` in glide3x.log → **stale DLL**: rebuild glide3x.dll from `Glide3xVK` HEAD and redeploy; game-side needs nothing.
3. DLL current and still failing → `d2boot.log` names the provider-init stage; a 12 s hangwatch dump names the wedged function (`d2exe.map` beside d2.exe for symbolization).
4. Field pass afterwards per H25 §6 glide bullets: world visible (not black) at the 1280x720 stop, fps overlay reads "Video: Glide", cutscenes resize 640x480 first (retail behavior), 800x600 stop byte-identical.

### 4.4 Root cause + fix (R46) — the first-frame divide-by-zero

- **Evidence re-read**: the wrapper log's "last glide calls = chroma/chromakeyValue" is the INIT-TIME `grChromakeyValue(0xff)` at `Glide.cpp:668` — the last init-state call of `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitWindow`, immediately before `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitTextureMemory()`. The 562 ms death window is inside InitTextureMemory (rev44's "first chroma'd image draw" reading is corrected).
- **Root cause**: `d6affdd` ("merge port", 2026-10-30) replaced the fec99ff-absorbed `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitTextureMemory` with the fork's pre-merge version, dropping the `nSubtileTexSize = 0x400` / `nFloorTexSize = 0x8000` assignments (retail values, mask-adjusted like the three surviving sizes). `GLFLOOR_Initialize`/`GLSUBTILE_Initialize` divide `(nVramEnd - nVramStart) / dwSize` (`glFloorCache.cpp:29`, `glSubtileCache.cpp:27`) by the uninitialized stack → **0xC0000094 when the slot reads 0** — build-layout-sensitive UB, which is why rev31's build ran fine and the rev41-era build died. Also dropped by the same commit: the 3.5 MB VRAM gate (log + `return 0`), the `RENDERER_GLIDE_InitDebugFont` call, the perspective branch's TMU≥2 split (`*3>>2` floor `0x1ccccc`), and the floor/subtile init sites were moved into the non-perspective branch (retail: perspective-only). Same commit also dropped the glide-side fix-3 class-slot guard and fix-2 stale-nodeIdx validation in `RENDERER_GLIDE_UploadTextureToCache` (comments kept, code deleted; D3D mirrors fixes 21/22 intact).
- **Provenance**: pickaxe `-S"nSubtileTexSize = 0x400"` — added at the original import `4b0154c`, removed only at `d6affdd`; present on 720p (:439–446, the "Glide works!" build), in `fec99ff`, and in upstream's current main. Marker audit across all ~100 d6affdd files found no further casualties (the Chat.cpp fix-15 flag is a file split — slider + clamp + comment intact in `EscMenu.cpp` :94/:101/:104). **d6affdd casualty list CLOSED**: sound (rev41), Inv.cpp shift order (rev44, adjacent port), glide texmem + fix-2/3 (this round).
- **Fix (R46)**: `scripts/rev46_glide_texmem_restore.py` — byte-exact restoration of InitTextureMemory + the fix-3/fix-2 guard blocks from `d6affdd~1` (spans extracted from git, no transcription; latin-1 byte-transparent; asserts + post-checks). Self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.6-R46`; 6 new gate rows pin the restored surface; **200/200**.
- **Field-verified (owner, 2026-10-31)**: rebuilt and run — owner verbatim: *"ok Glide i working now"*; documented publicly in **[issue #11](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/11)** ("[Video] Glide not working") via the closing-comment writeup (`ISSUE-11-CLOSE-COMMENT.md`). The 2026-10-30 crash file stands as the pre-fix record (own-code 0xC0000094, first frames, post-init chroma signature).

### 9.2 Track D — (720p) inventory misaligned — **FIELD-VERIFIED R45 — CLOSED**

- **Root cause (rev44)**: the fec99ff merge port placed every `VP_SHIFT_*` call BEFORE its paired `Get*Dimensions` fetch in `InitializeiInventoryPages` (and the `init_npc_coords` NPC block). The fetchers are pure overwrites (`TokTbls.cpp:806–894` — every field assigned from the inventory.txt row), so the shift landed on stale data and was clobbered: **all 40 table-rect sets stayed vanilla-800x600-absolute**. The panel art is drawn bottom-anchored by separate ui.cpp machinery, so the art moved (+240,+120 at 1280x720) while items, body slots, and hit-rects did not — measured off the owner's 720p screenshot: art grid cells at client x 657..947 (= 417+240, exact), HUD + orbs correctly shifted, potions drawn at vanilla-screen grid cols 8-9 row 1 over the paperdoll (240 left / 120 up of their cells).
- **Provenance**: `git diff 720p main --ignore-cr-at-eol` shows the flip pair-by-pair (720p = fetch→shift). The rev42 "site-5/6 machinery intact and identical" verdict examined the shift function bodies, not the call order — corrected here. (Also corrects this section's earlier "240/60" figure: the wide-UI Y offset at 720p is **120** = `ScreenSizeY-600`.)
- **Fix**: `scripts/rev44_inv_shift_order.py` — swaps all 40 pairs to fetch→shift; asserts counts (43 shift / 40 fetch / 40 calls) and per-pair target match. Gate: `site-5: fetch->shift pair order` (awk order check, ok=40/bad=0).
- **Field-verify ask**: 720p, open inventory with items in the grid — items must sit in their art cells; clicks land on the cells (hit-rects share the same structs); equipped items sit on their body-slot recesses. Same for vendor/stash/cube/trade pages if exercised.
- **R45**: owner confirmed — *"Inventory is aligned now"*. Track D CLOSED.

### 9.3 Track F — skills flyout opens on the wrong side of the screen — **FIELD-VERIFIED R46 — CLOSED**

- **Owner symptom (R45, verbatim)**: *"When I click the skills button in hotbar the menu the pops up is on the wrong side of the screen"* — reported right after the track-D field confirmation, i.e. at 1280x720 on the R44 build.
- **Root cause (R45)**: same class as track D — merge casualty at the call sites, half-applied flip. `SPELLSEL_RenderAllSkillGrids` (`D2Client/UI/SpellSel.cpp`) draws each flyout in an icon pass (which also registers the click regions via `SPELLSEL_RegisterSkillSlot`) and a tooltip pass. The Ghidra decomp-regen commits (`a91f7ac`/`aa93bfc`, post-site-5) scrambled the two icon-pass X anchors while both tooltip passes kept the correct wide-mode anchors:
  - right-hand grid icons: drew at the swap grid's anchor `0x50 + VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()` (x 320 at 720p — LEFT half) instead of `ScreenSizeX - VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX() - 0x80` (x 912 — above the hotbar);
  - swap-grid icons: drew at vanilla `0x50` (x 80, off the centered panel) instead of `0x50 + offset` (x 320);
  - the `nStartX` store feeding the right grid had lost its offset term (dead store — the regen also swapped the if/else bodies and flipped `bIsRightHand`, so the correct anchors were computed but never consumed).
  - Net field signature: icons + click regions on the left, tooltips on the right — the flyout "pops up on the wrong side".
- **Fix (R45)**: icon passes re-paired with their tooltip passes; `nStartX` offset restored. Anchor expressions are byte-identical to the last field-verified wide-mode state of this function (`git show 13aa82e~1`, pre-decomp-regen). Retail no-op: `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()` = 0 at 640x480/800x600. Y side untouched (`ScreenSizeY - 0x56` already bottom-anchored). Click handler `INPUT_SpellSelectClick` is region-driven — hit-testing follows automatically.
- **Gates (R45)**: spell right-grid icons bar-anchored (`nStartX` consumers = 2), swap-grid icons ride panel (`0x50 + offset` = 2), absence gate on the plain-`0x50` icon anchor, spell-grid offset count re-pinned 4→5. Suite: 194/194.
- **Field-verify ask**: 720p — click BOTH hotbar skill buttons: each flyout must hug the control panel above the skill buttons (icons, tooltips and clicks on the same cells); swap the left/right bindings and re-check both grids. 800x600: unchanged behavior.
- **R46**: owner confirmed — *"Skills Popout is fixed"*; the R45 fix shipped in release tag `D2LoD-Vanilla-Plus-0.0.6` (`0eb2655`). Track F CLOSED.

### 9.4 Track H — (720p) deposit-gold dialog misaligned

- **Owner symptom (verbatim)**: "[720p] Deposit Gold panel not aligned" (issue #12, 2026-10-31, on the field-verified R47 build) — the stash gold-coin modal opens misaligned; widgets off the window.
- **Root cause (R48) — third d6affdd casualty class: WRONG-BRANCH resolution.** The 720p fix (`cef3cf8`, Rev 32) rode five coord sites of `ui::UI_ShowGoldTransactionDialog` on `VPRES_GetWideUiOffsetX()/Y()`. Upstream's regeneration inverted the widget-alloc null-check shape (`if (!pAllocCtx) { gpX = 0; } else { construct }` → `if (pAllocCtx) { construct } else { gpX = 0; }` — decompiler-equivalent churn); the merge port resolved each VP-marked rider onto the constructor call that now sat in the FAILURE arm, immediately before the same-LHS null-out. All four widget riders became dead stores: normal play (alloc succeeds) always took the raw 800-space constants, so the dialog background (a site not inside a null-check) rode the wide offsets while every widget inside stayed vanilla-absolute — and on a failed alloc the misplaced call would pass a null ctx to the constructor (latent OOM null-deref) before the null-out ran.
- **Why every check passed**: marker + call + the x5 "site-6: gold dialog X shifts" count all green — presence ≠ placement ≠ **branch**.
- **Provenance**: `git show cef3cf8` (rider in the success arm of `if (!pAllocCtx)` on the 720p branch); main's inverted shape with the VP line in the failure arm arrived with the regen/merge chain.
- **Fix (R48)**: four riders moved into the alloc-success branches, else arms keep the null-out; R48 invariant comment at the spinner site; self-ID `Vanilla+0.0.6-R48`.
- **New instruments**: `scripts/rev48_wrong_branch_audit.py` — tree-wide sweep: any VP-marked assignment whose next non-empty line nulls the same LHS = dead-store casualty (fixed tree: 60 checked / 0 hits); gate `gold-dialog: riders in alloc-success branch (R48)` (awk order-check, ok=4/bad=0; negative-tested ok=0/bad=4 → FAIL). Gates: **202/202**.
- **Field-verify ask**: 720p — stash gold-coin click: deposit modal centered over the panel seam (X), riding bottom (Y), edit box / OK / Cancel / spinner aligned and clicking; repeat withdraw + the other entry points that open the same dialog; 800x600 parity: unchanged vs retail.

### 9.1 Track C — unique monsters uncolored / minion skeletons gold — **uniques FIELD-VERIFIED R49 CLOSED (issue #9); skeleton symptom ROOT-CAUGHT v2 + FIXED R51, field-verify pending (issue #8)**
- **Field-verified (owner, 2026-10-31)**: rebuilt and run — owner verbatim: *"R49 Field Tested, Confirmed Unique Monsters are Unique again."*; documented publicly in **[issue #9](https://github.com/VTSTech/D2LoD-libslang-vk/issues/9)** ("[Video] Unique Mobs don't have Unique Colors") via the closing-comment writeup (`ISSUE-9-CLOSE-COMMENT.md`). What closed: manifest symptom #2 (unique monsters base colors). The skeleton symptom (#3, issue #8) stayed open → R50 above.
- **R49 root cause (this round)**: `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` (`D2Client/GAME/PalShift.cpp:295`) early-returned null on a missing `palshift.dat` entry BEFORE the switch — but the rolled unique/champion tint indices 9-29 resolve only through the GLOBAL `RandTransforms.dat` table (30 × 0x100, `gbPalShiftRandTransformLoaded`), which needs no entry. Every random unique tinted → looked up through a dead path → base palette. Fix: entry-out deleted; identical guards moved into the case 0/1 and case 2-7 arms. Gate `palshift: rand-transform reachable (R49)`, suite 203/203. Full record: Rev 53.
- **Owner's proposal (issue #9 comment, 2026-10-30) evaluated and RETIRED**: its target mechanism (MonStats2 components → unit data) is intact end-to-end (loader `HDv`..`S8v` linkers → `MONSTER_RollComponentAppearance` in `MONSTER_InitStats` → 0xac wire with per-component widths derived from `aComponents` → client copy → composite). The wire-width math proves `aComponents[i]` is a variant COUNT, so the proposal's memcpy would write counts into index fields (out-of-range variants) — the correct mechanism already rolls. The proposal's SYMPTOM reading ("components never make it to the client") pointed at the right observable; the actual site was the tint lookup, one layer up. Provenance: all seven chain functions identical `44e3518` → main (`scripts/rev49_trackc_provenance.sh`).
- **Not a dropped fix** (verified rev42): main-vs-720p content-only diffs of `PalShift.cpp` / `Pallete.cpp` / `MonUnique.cpp` are decompiler-noise (guard inversions, `n > 0` ↔ `0 < n`, include order); no color fix exists anywhere in the H21 ledger. This is first-time work on upstream behavior — and R49 confirmed the defect is a BASELINE transcription miss, not a merge casualty.
- **Mechanism map (where retail colors live)**:
  - Static per-class palettes: `D2Client/UNIT/Monster.cpp:1238–1298` — default `TransLvl+2` (clamped <8), Andariel/Duriel (`0x9c`/`0xd3`) take difficulty palettes 3/4, `0x14d` → 5; structurally sane on main, **not** the suspect.
  - Random unique transforms (the gold/silver/red rolls): `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform` (`D2Client/GAME/PalShift.cpp:295`) — the `case 0/1: nClassId - 0x16bU` early-out and the `nPalShiftIndex - 8U` bound over the 30 global `RandTransforms.dat` entries (0x1e00 bytes, loaded once); **R49 fixed the entry-out that blocked this path** (see above); `PALSHIFT_LoadMonsterPalShift` (:209) loads per-monster `Data\Global\Monsters\<code>\Cof\palshift.dat` blobs (only special classes ship one); `PALSHIFT_InitPalShiftTable` (:275).
  - Draw-time application: the unit world-draw (`D2Client/Engine/Gfx.cpp:2925`) fetches `PALSHIFT_GetMonsterTransform(pUnit)` (when the unit has no act palette) and passes the 256-byte remap sub-table to `D2GFX_DrawImage(..., pPalette)` (:2996) per component layer — `GFXUTIL_GetItemColorForBodyLoc` passes monsters through unchanged (players only); `CUnit::SetPalShiftIndex`/getter (`D2Client/UNIT/CUnit.cpp:164-182`) store the index on `pGfxInfo->nPalShiftIndex`.
  - Unique seed → transform roll: `MonUnique.cpp` (`sNewSeed.nSeedLow % 100`); master→minion inheritance is the shared mechanism behind symptoms #2 and #3 — if the master's roll is lost but minions read a default, you get exactly "uniques plain, minions gold".
- **Pattern to hunt first (defect-12 precedent)**: a Ghidra transcription artifact pinning the transform index to one constant — "everything gold" is what a pinned index looks like. Audit the index math as constants/int-casts before touching logic.
- **Cheap first data ask**: one 800x600 D3D screenshot of a unique pack + whether ALL uniques are affected or act/class-specific; note whether champion (blue/orange) naming still works (same pack-generation path).
- **When the track opens**: sweep the PR branches (`jaenster-main`, `pr2`) for color-related fixes before writing anything; every fix lands with a gate row (§5 rule).
